1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the optimization space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 545 and the convergence rate is O(301/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 545 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the optimization structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 301. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the optimization structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 726. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 73 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. This completes the proof. QED.
